---

## **YOUR QUESTIONS**

"Where is the closest neighbor and how difficult is the county with building permits - they obviously put a shooting range out here so they were pretty flexible at some point. Who owns the adjacent land in all directions?"

---

## **CAROL'S RESPONSE**

She pulls out a folded property map from her folder.

**"Good questions. Let me show you."**

She unfolds the map on the hood of her Highlander.

---

### **NEIGHBORING PROPERTIES**

**"Closest occupied residence is here—"** 

She points to a spot on the map.

**"—about 1.8 miles northeast. Retired couple, the Hendersons. They run a small cattle operation, keep to themselves. Good neighbors according to the current owner."**

**"Next closest is 2.3 miles south. Also cattle ranching."**

She traces the property boundaries.

**"To the west and northwest - that's all BLM land. Bureau of Land Management. Federal grazing land, mostly empty except for occasional cattle leases. No development allowed."**

**"To the east, past the Hendersons, is more private ranch land. Large parcels, 100+ acres each. Very low density out here."**

**"South and southwest is a mix of private ranches and more BLM land."**

She looks up.

**"You're basically surrounded by ranch country and federal land. Very low chance of suburban sprawl reaching out here anytime soon."**

---

### **BUILDING PERMITS & COUNTY REGULATIONS**

Carol folds the map back up.

**"Cochise County is VERY flexible with building permits compared to Maricopa or Pima counties."**

**"For the shooting range - technically you don't even need a permit for earthwork on your own agricultural land as long as you're not altering drainage patterns. The owner just did it. No complaints, no issues."**

**"For structures:"**

**"Anything under 200 square feet - no permit required."**

**"Anything 200-1,000 square feet - basic permit, usually approved in 2-4 weeks, minimal fees ($200-$500)."**

**"Over 1,000 square feet or anything with plumbing - more involved but still easier than metro Phoenix. The county wants people to build out here. Tax base."**

She shrugs.

**"Setback requirements are minimal on land this size. As long as you're not building right on the property line, they don't care. Want to add a barn? A workshop? More shooting positions? Go for it."**

**"The only real restriction is septic - if you expand the house significantly or add a second dwelling, you might need a septic upgrade or second system. But that's health department, not building permits."**

---

### **ZONING**

**"Property is zoned RU-4."**

She sees your blank look.

**"Rural general use, 4-acre minimum parcel size. Means you can:"**

**"- Residential living"**

**"- Agricultural use"**

**"- Home business"**

**"- Recreational shooting"**

**"- Livestock"**

**"- Pretty much whatever you want as long as it's not heavy industrial."**

**"You could run a business out of the workshop - gunsmithing, training, whatever - and the county won't blink."**

She pauses.

**"Noise complaints? Not an issue out here. Your closest neighbor is almost 2 miles away and they're ranchers. They shoot too. The shooting range has been active for 15 years with zero complaints."**

---

## **CAROL CONTINUES**

**"Honestly, this is one of the most permissive counties in Arizona for property development. The owner built that entire range with no permit, no inspection, no issues. If you wanted to expand it, add structures, build training facilities - Cochise County will basically let you do whatever you want as long as it's safe."**

She smiles.

**"That's part of why people buy out here. Freedom to build and use your land how you want."**

---

## **NICK'S REACTION**

Nick leans over to you, quiet.

**"That's huge. We could build out whatever we need without red tape. Range expansion, obstacle courses, storage buildings, kennels - just do it."**

---

Shea is looking at the property boundaries on the map.

## **THE GLOOM BRIEFING - KELNA'S INTEL**

Based on indexed information:

### **WHAT IS THE GLOOM?**

- **Environmental corruption** (NOT necromantic like Thornmar Hollow)
- Started **3 months ago** at collapsed System dungeon **Ashveil Deep** (Tier 2, Uncommon)
- When dungeon collapsed, **containment failed** - something inside began **leaking**

### **PHYSICAL MANIFESTATION:**
- Thick, dark **fog/mist** - almost oily
- Spreads slowly (~50-100 meters per week)
- **Heavier than air** - pools in valleys, riverbeds, basements
- Currently **~3 kilometers diameter**, expanding steadily

### **ENVIRONMENTAL EFFECTS:**
- Plants **wither** within days
- Water turns **brackish**, undrinkable
- Animals **instinctively avoid** it

### **EFFECTS ON LIVING CREATURES:**
- **Short exposure (minutes)**: Headaches, nausea, disorientation
- **Medium exposure (hours)**: Weakness, vision problems, respiratory issues
- **Long exposure (days)**: Permanent damage - lung scarring, blindness, death

### **COMBAT THREATS:**
- **Gloom Hounds** (wolf-sized, shadowy)
- **Gloom Wraiths** (incorporeal, draining)
- **Gloom Stalkers** (humanoid, ambush predators)
- **Estimated levels**: 18-28, mostly Common to Uncommon tier

### **WHAT "STOPPING IT" ENTAILS:**

**STEP 1**: Navigate through 1-2 km of toxic fog
- Limited visibility (10-20 meters max)
- Constant environmental damage (HP drain, debuffs)
- Random monster spawns

**STEP 2**: Reach collapsed dungeon entrance
- Buried under rubble
- Heavy resistance near source (boss-tier monsters)

**STEP 3**: Seal the breach
- System will guide once inside
- Likely: destroy corrupted anchor, close rift, purge source node
- **Boss fight guaranteed**

**STEP 4**: Escape
- Sealing might trigger collapse/explosion/monster surge

### **KELNA'S RECOMMENDATION:**
**"Run it solo. Fast and quiet. Nick and Shea aren't ready for this environment."**

**REQUIRED EQUIPMENT:**
- Respirators/gas masks (airborne toxin)
- Healing potions (lots - constant HP drain)
- Magical light sources (better than torches in fog)
- Escape plan (portal scroll, emergency teleport)

**DISTANCE**: ~15 kilometers northeast of River's Bend
**TRAVEL TIME**: Half day each way + 1-2 hours fighting through fog
